//============================================================
//  KARGAS IV - Mission trigger notes
//  Required Locations: P1~P4 Start, P5 Base, P5 Home, P5 Cerebrate, P5 Area, P5 Scout, P5 Intruder,
//                      P5 Spawn1~4, P5 H1~4, P1~P4 Hunt, Player Destination, AttackPoint1,
//                      MD1~MD6,
//                      P6 Base, P6 Home, P6 Area, P6 Area Ground, P6 Area Air,
//                      P6 Scout, P6 Intruder, P6 Overmind, P6 Spawn1~4, P6 H1~8,
//                      P7 Base, P7 Home, P7 Area, P7 Area Ground, P7 Area Air,
//                      P7 Scout, P7 Intruder, P7 Overmind, P7 Spawn1~4, P7 H1~4,
//                      WestArea, East Area, Gas1, Gas2, Mission2 Gate1, Mission2 Gate3~4, NewRoute4~12, NewRoute14~15, Waypoint1~5,
//                      P6 Alert, P7 Alert, P6 Alert Ping, P7 Alert Ping
//
//  P6/P7 Overmind locations require preplaced invincible Overmind Cocoons
//  owned by Player 6 / Player 7. The event disables invincibility on the selected one.
//
//  Timer rule: 12 DC tick = 1 second
//    10s = 120, 60s = 720, 90s = 1080, 3min = 2160, 4min = 2880, 5min = 3600
//
//  Player 8 DC roles
//    Terran Civilian      : zerg resource supply timer
//    Terran Marine        : P5 base defense cooldown
//    Terran Armory        : P6 base defense cooldown
//    Protoss Zealot       : P7 base defense cooldown
//    Terran Valkyrie      : mission phase flag (1=M1, 3=M2, 4=clear)
//    Terran Vulture       : mission2 elapsed timer
//    Protoss Probe        : P5 scout rotation index
//    Protoss Scout        : P6 scout rotation index
//    Protoss Carrier      : P7 scout rotation index
//    Terran Barracks      : P5 Hunt all-out attack timer
//    Terran Firebat       : P6 Hunt all-out attack timer
//    Protoss Arbiter      : P7 Hunt all-out attack timer
//    Terran Goliath       : P5 scout overlord cycle timer
//    Protoss Archon       : P6 scout overlord cycle timer
//    Protoss Dark Templar : P7 scout overlord cycle timer
//    Protoss Shuttle      : P1~P4 Hunt location refresh timer
//    Terran Wraith        : mission1 late-wave cooldown / P6 mission2 early alert defense recharge timer
//    Terran Battlecruiser : P5 overlord supply cycle timer
//    Protoss Dragoon      : P6 overlord supply cycle timer
//    Protoss High Templar : P7 overlord supply cycle timer
//    Terran Science Vessel: player alliance refresh timer
//    Terran Medic         : P6 intrusion Overlord dispatch timer
//    Terran SCV           : P7 intrusion Overlord dispatch timer
//    Terran Academy       : difficulty selection flag (0=unset, 1=easy, 2=normal, 3=hard)
//    Terran Ghost         : difficulty reminder timer
//    Terran Dropship      : mission1 late-wave variant index / P7 mission2 early alert defense recharge timer
//    Protoss Corsair      : mission1 outer colony warning cooldown timer
//    Zerg Creep Colony    : mission1 P6/P7 creep colony warning counter
//    Zerg Sunken Colony   : P6 Hatchery/Lair/Hive losses for final scream assault
//    Zerg Spore Colony    : P7 Hatchery/Lair/Hive losses for final scream assault
//    Zerg Zergling        : P6 mission2 penalty assault cooldown
//    Zerg Hydralisk       : P7 mission2 penalty assault cooldown
//    Zerg Lurker          : P6 mission2 penalty assault ready flag (0=idle, 1=spawning, 2=relay, 3=warning, 4=weakened warning, 5=weakened spawning, 6=unused, 7=weakened pre-analysis queued)
//    Zerg Mutalisk        : P7 mission2 penalty assault ready flag (0=idle, 1=spawning, 2=relay, 3=warning, 4=weakened warning, 5=weakened spawning, 6=unused, 7=weakened pre-analysis queued)
//    Zerg Ultralisk       : P6 mission2 penalty assault spawn step timer / pre-roll cue pulse while ready flag is idle
//    Zerg Devourer        : P7 mission2 penalty assault spawn step timer / pre-roll cue pulse while ready flag is idle
//    Zerg Scourge         : P6 mission2 penalty assault waypoint window timer
//    Zerg Guardian        : P7 mission2 penalty assault waypoint window timer
//    Zerg Drone           : P6 mission2 penalty assault relay order cooldown
//    Zerg Defiler         : P7 mission2 penalty assault relay order cooldown
//    Protoss Reaver       : P6 mission2 penalty assault spawn stage index
//    Protoss Observer     : P7 mission2 penalty assault spawn stage index
//    Protoss Dark Archon : mission2 final scream assault preparation timer
//
//  Player 5 DC roles
//    Zerg Hatchery        : mission1 late-wave cooldown seed
//    Zerg Lair            : mission1 late-wave cooldown seed
//    Zerg Hive            : mission1 late-wave cooldown seed
//    Terran Marine        : P5 mission2 early Sunken Colony loss warning progress
//    Terran Firebat       : P5 mission2 early Spore Colony loss warning progress
//
//  Switch roles
//    Switch1 : defeat guard
//    Switch7 : mission1 multi scan revealers created
//    Switch8 : mission1 multi scan revealers removed
//    Switch9/10 : P6 mission2 penalty assault random selector
//    Switch11/12: P7 mission2 penalty assault random selector
//    Switch13   : mission1 P6/P7 creep colony warning display flag
//    Switch14~16: P6 mission2 penalty assault random waypoint selector
//    Switch17~19: P7 mission2 penalty assault random waypoint selector
//    Switch20/21: P6 mission2 penalty assault random Hunt selector
//    Switch22/23: P7 mission2 penalty assault random Hunt selector
//    Switch24   : P6 mission1 outer colony resource unlocked
//    Switch25   : P7 mission1 outer colony resource unlocked
//    Switch26   : mission2 first penalty assault order shuffle
//    Switch27/28: P6 mission2 penalty assault cooldown jitter
//    Switch29/30: P7 mission2 penalty assault cooldown jitter
//    Switch31   : P6 MainBase tech collapse main-assault lock
//    Switch32   : P7 MainBase tech collapse main-assault lock
//    Switch33   : P6 mission1 main assault cooldown enabled
//    Switch34   : P7 mission1 main assault cooldown enabled
//    Switch35   : P6 mission1 first tech-core assault boost used
//    Switch36   : P7 mission1 first tech-core assault boost used
//    Switch37   : P6 one-colony clear reward/message paid
//    Switch38   : P7 one-colony clear reward/message paid
//    Switch39   : mission2 overmind cocoon side selector (clear=P6, set=P7)
//    Switch40   : mission2 overmind cocoon active / reward unpaid
//    Switch41   : mission2 overmind cocoon weakened-assault pending
//    Switch42/43: P5 hunt all-out random target selector
//    Switch44/45: P6 hunt all-out random target selector
//    Switch46/47: P7 hunt all-out random target selector
//    Switch50   : mission2 assault setup initialized
//    Switch51   : P6 mission2 penalty assault relay pulse open
//    Switch52   : P7 mission2 penalty assault relay pulse open
//    Switch53   : P6 mission2 penalty assault spawn rally pulse open
//    Switch54   : P7 mission2 penalty assault spawn rally pulse open
//    Switch55   : P6 AI activation requested
//    Switch56   : P7 AI activation requested
//    Switch58   : P5 Hive death ground counterattack used
//    Switch59   : P6 mission2 early alert defense charge ready
//    Switch60   : P7 mission2 early alert defense charge ready
//    Switch61/62: P6 mission2 pre-roll wave selector
//    Switch63   : P6 mission2 pre-roll active
//    Switch64   : P6 mission2 pre-roll message shown
//    Switch65/66: P7 mission2 pre-roll wave selector
//    Switch67   : P7 mission2 pre-roll active
//    Switch68   : P7 mission2 pre-roll message shown
//    Switch69   : P6 intrusion detected for Overlord dispatch
//    Switch70   : P7 intrusion detected for Overlord dispatch
//    Switch71   : mission1 P6 outer colony awakening warning latch
//    Switch72   : mission1 P7 outer colony awakening warning latch
//    Switch73   : mission1 both outer colonies awakening warning latch
//    Switch74   : P6 collapsed; P7 final scream assault active
//    Switch75   : P7 collapsed; P6 final scream assault active
//    Switch76   : mission2 final scream assault intro message shown
//    Switch77   : mission2 final scream assault 30s warning shown
//    Switch78   : mission2 final scream assault launch message shown
//    Switch79   : mission2 final scream assault batch 1 spawned
//    Switch80   : mission2 final scream assault batch 2 spawned
//    Switch81   : mission2 final scream assault batch 3 spawned
//    Switch82   : mission2 final scream assault batch 4 spawned
//    Switch83   : mission2 final scream assault batch 5 spawned
//    Switch84   : P6 base defense suspended during final scream assault
//    Switch85   : P7 base defense suspended during final scream assault
//    Switch86   : mission2 final scream assault batch 6 spawned
//    Switch87   : mission2 final scream assault batch 7 spawned
//    Switch88   : mission2 final scream assault batch 8 spawned
//    Switch89   : mission2 final scream assault batch 9 spawned
//    Switch90   : mission2 final scream assault batch 10 spawned
//    Switch91   : Gas1 P5 Overmind Cocoon confirmed
//    Switch92   : Gas1 P5 Overmind Cocoon gas reward paid
//    Switch93   : Gas2 P5 Overmind Cocoon confirmed
//    Switch94   : Gas2 P5 Overmind Cocoon gas reward paid
//    Switch95   : P6 mission2 tech-structure acceleration info shown
//    Switch96   : P7 mission2 tech-structure acceleration info shown
//    Switch97~100  : P1~P4 P5 first Mutalisk warning shown
//    Switch101~104 : P1~P4 P5 first Guardian warning shown
//    Switch105~108 : P1~P4 P5 first Devourer warning shown
//    Switch109~112 : P1~P4 P5 early Sunken Colony decay warning queued
//    Switch113~116 : P1~P4 P5 early Spore Colony decay warning queued
//    Switch117     : difficulty selector spawned / selection window active
//    Switch118     : P6 mission2 first main assault has started
//    Switch119     : P7 mission2 first main assault has started
//    Switch120     : P6 mission2 difficulty cooldown boost applied for current cycle
//    Switch121     : P7 mission2 difficulty cooldown boost applied for current cycle
//    Switch122     : mission2 difficulty extra Hatcheries spawned
//    Switch123     : easy difficulty reminder queued
//    Switch124     : normal difficulty reminder queued
//    Switch125     : hard difficulty reminder queued
//    Switch126     : difficulty reminder displayed to players; clear queued reminder on P8 pass
//
//  Map editor settings
//    Player 5/6/7 Overlord upgrades must be configured in SCMDraft:
//    Pneumatized Carapace and Antennae = Level 1 + Researched.
//
//  Note: avoid Order("...", "Men", ...) for broad combat orders.
//============================================================
// 게임 시작 브리핑
Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, " ");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>작전 개시: 카르가스 IV");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Set Mission Objectives("<03>미션 1: 첫 전선\n\n<04>- 목표: 북서쪽 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락 섬멸\n- 클리어 조건: 북서쪽 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락의 해처리 / 레어 / 하이브 모두 파괴");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Sunken Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Spore Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Terran Marine", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Terran Firebat", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Ghost", Set To, 0);
	Set Switch("Switch109", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch110", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch111", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch112", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch113", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch114", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch115", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch116", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch117", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch118", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch119", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch120", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch121", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch122", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch123", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch124", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch125", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch126", clear);
}

}

Trigger("All Players"){

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Difficulty selection
//
//  Player 8 / Terran Academy:
//    0 = unset, 1 = easy, 2 = normal, 3 = hard
//  Switch117 = selector civilian has been created.
//  Selector civilian priority: lowest active player slot receives it immediately.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
	Switch("Switch117", Not Set);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Terran Civilian", 1, "Selector");
	Set Switch("Switch117", set);
}

Trigger("Player 2"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
	Switch("Switch117", Not Set);
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Terran Civilian", 1, "Selector");
	Set Switch("Switch117", set);
}

Trigger("Player 3"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
	Switch("Switch117", Not Set);
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At most, 0);
	Command("Player 2", "Buildings", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Terran Civilian", 1, "Selector");
	Set Switch("Switch117", set);
}

Trigger("Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
	Switch("Switch117", Not Set);
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At most, 0);
	Command("Player 2", "Buildings", At most, 0);
	Command("Player 3", "Buildings", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Terran Civilian", 1, "Selector");
	Set Switch("Switch117", set);
}

Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 55);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 57);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Exactly, 0);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Minimap Ping("Selector");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>난이도를 선택하세요. <04>선택 시민을 <07>쉬움<04>, <11>보통<04>, <08>어려움<04> 구역으로 이동시키십시오. 선택하지 않으면 <07>쉬움<04>으로 진행됩니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>[난이도 차이 안내]");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<11>보통: <04>적 공세 간격이 짧아지고, 적 해처리가 추가됩니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<08>어려움: <04>적 공세 간격이 더 짧아지고, 적 병력과 해처리가 더 늘어납니다.");
}

Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 115);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 117);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Exactly, 0);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Minimap Ping("Selector");

}

}

	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<04>선택한 난이도는 <08>어려움<04>입니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<08>어려움 효과: <04>적 공세 간격 추가 감소, 적 병력과 해처리 증가.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>행운을 빕니다!");
}

// Mission 1 late-wave cooldown starts after 15 minutes.

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 900);
	Command("Player 5", "Zerg Cerebrate", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Mission 2 main assault cooldowns advance during mission2.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// If an outer colony tech core is attacked during mission1, only that
// colony's main-assault cooldown starts advancing early.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch33", Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch34", Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Once an assault is queued, its spawn/relay timers may run in mission1 or mission2,
// but stop after the mission is complete.

	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Preserve Trigger();

	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 0, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, gas);

//  P6: Protoss Archon / Protoss Scout
//  P7: Protoss Dark Templar / Protoss Carrier
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

//한 사이클 돌아가게 세팅
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 175);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", Set To, 2160);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Archon", Set To, 2160);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dark Templar", Set To, 2160);
}

// Index 0 → P1 Hunt
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 180);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", At least, 1440);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Probe", Exactly, 0);
Actions:
	Move Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", 1, "P5 Base", "P5 Scout");
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", "P5 Scout", "P1 Hunt", Move);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", Subtract, 1440);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Probe", Set To, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Elapsed Time(At least, 180);

	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Scout", Set To, 1);

}

Conditions:

//  11분 럴커 공습 이후, 15분 대공습 이전에 P1~P4 멀티를 2분간 정찰.

// 멀티 위치 궤도 스캔 - 12분 30초
Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 750);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Minimap Ping("P1 Multi");
	Minimap Ping("P2 Multi");
	Minimap Ping("P3 Multi");
	Minimap Ping("P4 Multi");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>궤도 스캔 갱신. 각 전선 외곽에 아직 확보하지 않은 자원이 있습니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>본진 자원만으론 오래 못 버틴다. 여력이 생기면 추가 자원을 확보해라.");
}

	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P2 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P3 Multi");

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - P5 Hive death ground counterattack
//
//  Fires once when a P5 Hive death is detected, before the late-wave
//  reward trigger consumes the P5 Zerg Hive death counter.
//  Composition: 64 Zerglings + 16 Hydralisks + 2 Ultralisks + 2 Defilers
//  = 60 Zerg supply.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Hive", At least, 1);
	Switch("Switch58", Not Set);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2500);
	Minimap Ping("P5 Home");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>하이브 붕괴 확인. 잠깐... 군락 내부 신호가 역류합니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>잔존 지상군이 한꺼번에 깨어난다. 전선을 유지해라.");
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Hive", At least, 1);
	Switch("Switch58", Not Set);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 16, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 4, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", 1, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit with Properties("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", 1, "P5 Spawn1", 1);
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 16, "P5 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 4, "P5 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 16, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 4, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", 1, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit with Properties("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", 1, "P5 Spawn3", 1);
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 16, "P5 Spawn4");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 4, "P5 Spawn4");
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "P5 Spawn1", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "P5 Spawn1", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "P5 Spawn1", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "P5 Spawn1", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "P5 Spawn2", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "P5 Spawn2", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "P5 Spawn3", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "P5 Spawn3", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "P5 Spawn3", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "P5 Spawn3", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "P5 Spawn4", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "P5 Spawn4", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Set To, 720);
	Set Switch("Switch58", set);
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 difficulty adjustment - Normal
//
//  Player 8 / Terran Academy = 2 normal, 3 hard.
//  Normal adjustment also applies to hard unless noted otherwise.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// Wave 2 normal reinforcement - 8:00 launch group.
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 450);
	Command("Player 5", "Buildings", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 1, "P5 Spawn4");
}

// Wave 3 normal reinforcement - 11:00 launch group.
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 630);
	Command("Player 5", "Buildings", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 1, "P5 Spawn4");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Lurker", 1, "P5 Spawn4");
}

// Colony destruction penalty: +5 seconds beyond easy.

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Hatchery", At least, 1);

	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Exactly, 2);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", At least, 2400);

	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  늦은 웨이브 4종 변형 (15분 후, 150초마다 순환)
//  Wraith DC = 쿨다운 (2520틱 = 210s)
//  Dropship DC = 변형 인덱스 (0/1/2/3)
//
//  변형 A: 지상 스웜 (저글링/히드라/럴커 대량)
//  변형 B: 공중 하라스 (뮤탈/스커지)
//  변형 C: 중장갑 돌파 (울트라/디파일러/히드라)
//  변형 D: 공성 공중 (가디언/디바우러/뮤탈)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// 군락 테크 건물 붕괴 반격 보정 (1변종 = 2520틱, 시작 보정 = 10080틱)
// Seed enough cooldown for four late-wave variants when late waves begin.

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 895);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", Set To, 10080);
}

}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - dynamic building tracking and random all-out attack
//
//  Protoss Shuttle DC = Hunt location refresh timer (10s = 120)
//  Terran Barracks DC = P5 all-out attack timer (60s = 720)
//  Switch42/43 = P5 random Hunt target selector
//    clear/clear = P1, set/clear = P2, clear/set = P3, set/set = P4.
//  If a dead player is selected, the timer is not reset and rolls again.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - outer colony attack penalty
//
//  If players bypass the first colony and attack P6/P7 during mission1,
//  destroyed outer-colony structures feed resources back into that colony.
//  If either outer colony's Hatchery/Lair/Hive collapses, both P6 and P7 wake up.
//  The first tech-core collapse also primes one large assault from that colony only.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// Repeat the outer-colony intrusion warning at most once every 20 seconds.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", Subtract, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", Exactly, 0);
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Alert", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Alert", "P6 Alert Ping");
	Minimap Ping("P6 Alert Ping");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>선봉대, 이곳이 아니다. 북서쪽 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락으로 병력을 돌려 공격해라.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<06>경고: 미션1 진행중 <11>주황색 저그<06>를 제외한 적을 공격할 경우 해당 적이 깨어납니다.");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", Set To, 240);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Player-facing warning messages. Detection below is owned by Player 8 so
// global switches cannot suppress P2~P4 copies before they display.
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch71", Set);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>미확인 군락의 자원 흐름이 열렸습니다. 생체 반응이 급격히 증가합니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>방금 선을 넘었다. 저 군락은 이제 우리를 향해 전력을 모을 거다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<06>경고: 이 군락이 깨어났습니다. 공격을 계속하면 미션2에 시작되는 공세가 미션1에 시작됩니다.");
}

	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 14040);

// Count destroyed P6/P7 creep colonies together and warn every 3 kills.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch13", Set);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch13", clear);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch13", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Creep Colony", At least, 3);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Creep Colony", Subtract, 3);
	Set Switch("Switch13", set);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch13", Set);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>선봉대, 병력 분산하지 마라. 목표는 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락이다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<06>경고: <04>미션1에 <11>주황색 저그<04>가 아닌 다른 적을 공격하면 난이도가 상승합니다.");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Creep Colony", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);

	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Creep Colony", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Creep Colony", Subtract, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 7", "Zerg Creep Colony", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Creep Colony", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 7", "Zerg Creep Colony", Subtract, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Sunken Colony", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);

	Set Switch("Switch55", set);
	Set Resources("Player 6", Add, 500, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 6", Add, 250, gas);
	Set Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Sunken Colony", Subtract, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - Zerg gas store rewards
//  Switch91 : Gas1 P5 Overmind Cocoon confirmed
//  Switch92 : Gas1 P5 Overmind Cocoon gas reward paid
//  Switch93 : Gas2 P5 Overmind Cocoon confirmed
//  Switch94 : Gas2 P5 Overmind Cocoon gas reward paid
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch91", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 5", "Overmind Cocoon", "Gas1", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch91", set);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch93", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 5", "Overmind Cocoon", "Gas2", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch93", set);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch91", Set);
	Switch("Switch92", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 5", "Overmind Cocoon", "Gas1", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 1", Add, 500, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 500, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 3", Add, 500, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 4", Add, 500, gas);
	Set Switch("Switch92", set);
}

}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  패배 - 단계와 관계없이 미션1/2 어느 단계든 본진 파괴 시 패배.
//  Valkyrie >= 1 (게임 시작 후) + Elapsed Time 가드로 초기 전환 보호
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Command("Current Player", "Buildings", At most, 0);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 60);
	Switch("Switch1", Not Set);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\OutOfGas.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>작전 실패: 전선 붕괴");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>무너진 건 방어선이지, 부대가 아니다. 교신 유지하고 잔존 병력은 후방으로 빠져라.");
	Wait(1500);
	Defeat();
}

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 12);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Sunken Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Spore Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Terran Marine", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Terran Firebat", Set To, 0);
	Set Switch("Switch109", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch110", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch111", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch112", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch113", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch114", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch115", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch116", clear);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

//============================================================
//  Player-claimable missile turrets
//  Preplace one Player 9 Terran Missile Turret at each MD1~MD6 location.
//============================================================

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Bring("Current Player", "Men", "MD1", At least, 1);
	Bring("Player 9", "Terran Missile Turret", "MD1", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Current Player", "Terran Missile Turret", 1, "MD1");
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>전술 지원: <04>미사일 터렛 제어권 확보.");
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Bring("Current Player", "Men", "MD2", At least, 1);
	Bring("Player 9", "Terran Missile Turret", "MD2", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Current Player", "Terran Missile Turret", 1, "MD2");
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>전술 지원: <04>미사일 터렛 제어권 확보.");
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Base defense response
//
//  P5: Terran Marine DC, P6: Terran Armory DC, P7: Protoss Zealot DC
//  P5 cooldown: 10s = 120 ticks. P6/P7 cooldown: 7s = 84 ticks.
//
//  P5 uses P5 Area for detection and P5 Intruder as the moving target.
//  P6/P7 Area Ground/Air locations are map-side filtered. Each response
//  moves the single Intruder location onto the detected unit, then orders
//  the matching defenders to patrol there. Home-defense triggers mark
//  intrusion flags; separate 20s dispatch triggers send one existing
//  Overlord per colony. Allied-base support does not mark Overlord flags
//  or overwrite a pending home-intrusion Overlord target.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P5 Area", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P5 Area", "P5 Intruder");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", Set To, 0);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Lurker", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Mutalisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Scourge", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Guardian", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Devourer", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Queen", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Infested Terran", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Bring("Player 2", "Men", "P5 Area", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 2", "Men", "P5 Area", "P5 Intruder");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", Set To, 0);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);

// P6/P7 use separate Ground/Air area locations for detection. The single
// Intruder location is moved onto the detected player unit and then used
// as the patrol target. Triggers stay owned by the defending computer
// player, while P1~P4 are checked explicitly.

// P6: computer-owned defense triggers. Area Ground/Air locations filter target height.

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Armory", At least, 84);
	Switch("Switch84", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Area Ground", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Area Ground", "P6 Intruder");
	Set Switch("Switch69", set);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Hydralisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Lurker", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Mutalisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Ultralisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Guardian", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Queen", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Defiler", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Infested Terran", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// P6 allied-base support: after all P6 self-defense triggers so P6 home defense
// consumes the shared cooldown first when both bases are under attack.
// Only support an allied base while that base area still contains at least one building.

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Armory", At least, 84);
	Switch("Switch84", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch69", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 7", "Buildings", "P7 Area Ground", At least, 1);
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P7 Area Ground", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P7 Area Ground", "P6 Intruder");
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Hydralisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Lurker", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Mutalisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Ultralisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Guardian", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission-independent - dynamic building tracking and random all-out attack
//
//  Protoss Shuttle DC = Hunt location refresh timer (10s = 120)
//  Terran Firebat DC = P6 all-out attack timer (60s = 720)
//  Protoss Arbiter DC = P7 all-out attack timer (60s = 720)
//  Switch44/45 = P6 random Hunt target selector
//  Switch46/47 = P7 random Hunt target selector
//    clear/clear = P1, set/clear = P2, clear/set = P3, set/set = P4.
//  If a dead player is selected, the timer is not reset and rolls again.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 - P6/P7 uncaptured-colony penalty assaults
//
//  12 DC tick = 1 second. Easy base cooldown is 1260s = 15120 ticks.
//  Roll triggers subtract only the cooldown they consumed, so excess
//  progress from repeated Hatchery/Lair/Hive kills stays queued.
//  Hatchery/Lair/Hive kills add 70s worth of cooldown progress.
//
//  Waves may fire during mission1 or mission2, but not after victory.
//  They are rolled randomly, then spawned across Spawn1~4 in parallel
//  timed batches to reduce blocked creation space. Spawn timers only count up;
//  separate stage counters prevent repeated or chained creation. Air-heavy
//  waves are split into smaller sub-batches. Spawned groups
//  rally to Waypoint1~5. For 120s after the final batch,
//  P6/P7 assault units reaching the selected waypoint redirect
//  toward the already selected P1~P4 Hunt location.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// Reset assault counters once on mission2 entry.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 12);
	Switch("Switch50", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch26", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch27", set);
	Set Switch("Switch28", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch29", set);
	Set Switch("Switch30", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch37", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch38", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch39", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch40", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch41", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch61", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch62", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch63", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch64", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch65", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch66", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch67", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch68", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch74", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch75", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch76", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch77", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch78", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch79", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch80", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch81", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch82", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch83", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch84", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch85", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch86", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch87", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch88", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch89", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch90", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch118", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch119", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch120", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch121", clear);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dark Archon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Sunken Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Spore Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Devourer", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Observer", Set To, 0);
}

// First penalty assaults are staggered after the mission2 assault cooldown opens
// at Vulture 756: one colony spawns at about 5:00 mission2 elapsed, the other at
// about 8:00. From the cooldown-open point, those are 3:47 and 6:47.
// Roll warnings fire 10 seconds before the first spawn batch.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 12);
	Switch("Switch50", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch26", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Set To, 12396);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);

	Switch("Switch26", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch32", Not Set);

}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:

	Switch("Switch26", Set);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);

}

// If a colony's MainBase tech core collapses, lock that colony's main assault.
// This runs after the first-assault stagger setup so a collapsed colony stays at 0.
Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Hatchery", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Lair", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Hive", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Switch("Switch61", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch62", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch63", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch64", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch31", set);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 7"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch32", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 7", "Zerg Hatchery", "P7 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 7", "Zerg Lair", "P7 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 7", "Zerg Hive", "P7 MainBase", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Devourer", Set To, 0);
	Set Switch("Switch65", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch66", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch67", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch68", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch32", set);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// First single-colony clear rewards. Require the opposite tech core to still
// exist so simultaneous P6/P7 collapses go straight to the victory sequence.
Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch31", Set);
	Switch("Switch32", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch37", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 7", "Zerg Hatchery", "P7 MainBase", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 1", Add, 2000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 2000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 3", Add, 2000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 4", Add, 2000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 1", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 3", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 4", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Switch("Switch37", set);
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 difficulty timing adjustments
//
//  Easy keeps the existing first-assault seed and 20:30/21:00/21:30
//  repeated cooldowns. Normal and hard only advance cooldown progress;
//  the existing roll, warning, 10-second spawn delay, and consume
//  triggers remain unchanged.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// First P6 assault seed: normal +30s, hard +60s.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 12);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Exactly, 2);
	Switch("Switch50", Set);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At least, 10236);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At most, 12396);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 360);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 12);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch50", Set);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At least, 10236);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At most, 12396);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 720);
}

// before the normal random roll files can consume it.
// Pre-rolls show an early analysis cue; fallback rolls preserve the selected timing if acceleration skips that window.

	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At most, 14759);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Exactly, 0);
	Switch("Switch27", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch28", Not Set);

	Set Switch("Switch15", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch16", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch20", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch21", randomize);

	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  P6 main-assault roll with safe wave-only pre-roll
//  Pre-roll stores only the upcoming wave in dedicated switches.
//  Route, Hunt target, and assault state are decided at the original warning timing.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// P6 wave-only pre-rolls fire 80 seconds before the original warning timing.
// P6 pre-roll wave - cooldown 20m30s - Hive active
Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Command("Player 6", "Zerg Hive", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At least, 13800);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At most, 14759);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Exactly, 0);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch63", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch27", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch28", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch61", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch62", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch63", set);
	Set Switch("Switch64", clear);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Subtract, 15120);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Assault warning delay. Roll triggers show warning first, then spawn begins after 10 seconds.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Devourer", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Devourer", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Observer", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 difficulty wave-size scaling
//
//  Normal adds about +10% over easy. Hard adds another small layer so
//  hard totals about +15% over easy. These triggers do not change
//  orders, waypoint relay timing, rewards, or Overlord support.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// P6 Wave 0 - Ultralisk + Zergling
Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Exactly, 0);
	Switch("Switch9", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch10", Not Set);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Ultralisk", 1, "P6 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", 6, "P6 Spawn1");
	Create Unit with Properties("Player 6", "Zerg Defiler", 1, "P6 Spawn2", 2);
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", 5, "P6 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Ultralisk", 1, "P6 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", 5, "P6 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", 5, "P6 Spawn4");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 7"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Exactly, 2);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", At least, 721);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Defiler", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Observer", Set To, 0);
	Set Switch("Switch52", clear);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 - hatchery tech loss accelerates main assaults
//
//  Hatchery/Lair/Hive kills reduce that colony's own main assault
//  cooldown by difficulty and grant players minerals/gas.
//  12 DC tick = 1 second: easy 70s = 840, normal 80s = 960, hard 90s = 1080.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 6", "Zerg Hatchery", At least, 1);
	Switch("Switch95", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch95", set);
}

	Set Switch("Switch95", set);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 - final scream assault after one colony collapses
//
//  If one P6/P7 MainBase tech core fully collapses while the other
//  still survives and has lost 8 Hatchery/Lair/Hive tech structures,
//  the remaining colony prepares one final 500-supply distributed
//  assault. 12 DC ticks = 1 second.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// P6 collapsed first; P7 prepares the final assault.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch31", Set);
	Switch("Switch32", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Spore Colony", At least, 8);
	Switch("Switch74", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch75", Not Set);

	Set Switch("Switch74", set);
	Set Switch("Switch76", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch77", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch78", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch79", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch80", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch81", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch82", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch83", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch84", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch85", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch86", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch87", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch88", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch89", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch90", clear);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dark Archon", Set To, 0);
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  터보 트리거 (Hyper Trigger) × 4 - Player 8 소유
//
//  원리: Wait 액션 직전에 모든 트리거 조건을 재검사 → Wait(0) 다수로
//        초당 ~12회 트리거 평가 강제. 12 DC tick = 1초 공식 성립.
//
//  4개 배치 이유 (NEO 갭 곱연산):
//    NEO 사이클 = (Wait 개수 + 1) ^ (트리거 개수)
//    1개: 63 사이클 ≈ 5초마다 끊김 (사실상 무효)
//    4개: 63^4 ≈ 1575만 사이클 ≈ 15일 이상 무중단 → 어떤 게임 길이도 안전
//
//  Player 8 단독 소유: 다른 Wait를 사용하는 트리거가 P8에 없음을 확인.
//  P8은 컴퓨터 중립 슬롯이라 게임 도중 이탈 위험 없음.
//
//  주의: P8에는 Wait를 사용하는 다른 트리거를 추가하지 말 것
//        (Wait 블록 발생 시 hyper 효과 멈춤).
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Always();
Actions:
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
	Wait(0);
